- **option-pricing**: Pricing completo de opciones europeas y americanas. 9 metodos: Black-Scholes, Binomial CRR, Trinomial, Monte Carlo (antithetic) + Longstaff-Schwartz, Bjerksund-Stensland 2002 / BAW (American closed-form), Heston 1993 (vol estocastica, sonrisa via Fourier), Bates 1996 (Heston + Merton jumps, crash risk), greeks (BS), implied vol, P(ITM) y P(Profit).
